# original atlantis2.exe (French CD build of 1999, 2199552 bytes) under emulation, its analyser
# (0x0059f720) and evaluator (0x0059fa40) called directly (no head), the voice decoded by its APC
# decoder (0x00593d00 / 0x00593d20 / 0x00593e30), the speakers' numbers read by its atof
# (0x005ae620), x87 control word 0x027f; frames every 1/15 s from t = Offset (t =
# float32(k / 15 + Offset), k = 0 .. int(samples / 22050 * 15) + 1).
# Derived values only: counts and MD5s. Binary atlantis2-cd-fr-2199552 (MD5 5e218f7fa9754532ae1d7a4275443fef).
# Stubs: operator new / malloc / free (a bump heap) and FUN_0046a810, the morph-key lookup.
# Per line: speaker, kind (head / voixoff / voixoff0 / cinema), samples, frames, frames whose
# weights are NaN (first weight 0xffc00000), the first such frame, the MD5 of all the weights
# (7 little-endian float words a frame); "apc missing" when no disc has the voice, "matrix none"
# for a speaker without a [Matrice].
